- `AstVisitor ast_visitor_visit(AstVisitor v, AstNode node)` – Visitor.Visit.
- `void ast_walk(AstVisitor v, AstNode node)` – Walk traverses an AST in depth-first order: It starts by calling v.Visit(node); node must not be nil.
- `void ast_inspect(AstNode node, AstInspectFunc f)` – Inspect traverses an AST in depth-first order: It starts by calling f(node); node must not be nil.
- `IterSeq ast_preorder(AstNode root)` – Preorder returns an iterator over all the nodes of the syntax tree beneath (and including) the specified root, in depth-first preorder.
- `void ast_preorder_stack(Alloc *a, AstNode root, Slice stack, AstPreorderStackFunc f)` – PreorderStack traverses the tree rooted at root, calling f before visiting each node.
- `AstDirective ast_parse_directive(TokenPos pos, Str c, bool *ok)` – ParseDirective parses a single comment line for a directive comment.
- `TokenPos ast_directive_pos(AstDirective *d)`
- `TokenPos ast_directive_end(AstDirective *d)`
- `Slice ast_directive_parse_args(AstDirective *d, Alloc *a, Error *err)` – ParseArgs parses a Directive's arguments using the standard convention, which is a sequence of tokens, where each token may be a bare word, or a double quoted Go string, or a back quoted raw Go string.
- `Str ast_obj_kind_string(AstObjKind kind)` – ObjKind.String: "bad", "package", "const" and so on.
- `AstObject *ast_new_obj(Alloc *a, AstObjKind kind, Str name)` – NewObj creates a new object of a given kind and name.
- `TokenPos ast_object_pos(AstObject *obj)` – Pos computes the source position of the declaration of an object name.
- `AstScope *ast_new_scope(Alloc *a, AstScope *outer)` – NewScope creates a new scope nested in the outer scope.
- `AstObject *ast_scope_lookup(AstScope *s, Str name)` – Lookup returns the object with the given name if it is found in scope s, otherwise it returns nil.
- `AstObject *ast_scope_insert(AstScope *s, AstObject *obj)` – Insert attempts to insert a named object obj into the scope s.
- `Str ast_scope_string(AstScope *s, Alloc *a)` – Debugging support
- `AstPackage *ast_new_package(Alloc *a, TokenFileSet *fset, Map *files, AstImporter importer, AstScope *universe, Error *err)` – NewPackage creates a new Package node from a set of File nodes.
- `bool ast_file_exports(AstFile *src)` – FileExports trims the AST for a Go source file in place such that only exported nodes remain: all top-level identifiers which are not exported and their associated information (such as type, initial value, or function body) are removed.
- `bool ast_package_exports(AstPackage *pkg)` – PackageExports trims the AST for a Go package in place such that only exported nodes remain.
- `bool ast_filter_decl(AstDecl decl, AstFilter f)` – FilterDecl trims the AST for a Go declaration in place by removing all names (including struct field and interface method names, but not from parameter lists) that don't pass through the filter f.
- `bool ast_filter_file(AstFile *src, AstFilter f)` – FilterFile trims the AST for a Go file in place by removing all names from top-level declarations (including struct field and interface method names, but not from parameter lists) that don't pass through the filter f.
- `bool ast_filter_package(AstPackage *pkg, AstFilter f)` – FilterPackage trims the AST for a Go package in place by removing all names from top-level declarations (including struct field and interface method names, but not from parameter lists) that don't pass through the filter f.
- `AstFile *ast_merge_package_files(Alloc *a, AstPackage *pkg, AstMergeMode mode)` – MergePackageFiles creates a file AST by merging the ASTs of the files belonging to a package.
- `void ast_sort_imports(Alloc *a, TokenFileSet *fset, AstFile *f)` – SortImports sorts runs of consecutive import lines in import blocks in f.
- `bool ast_not_nil_filter(Str name, Any v)` – NotNilFilter is a FieldFilter that returns true for field values that are not nil; it returns false otherwise.
- `Error ast_fprint(Alloc *a, IoWriter w, TokenFileSet *fset, Any x, AstFieldFilter f)` – Fprint prints the (sub-)tree starting at AST node x to w.
- `Error ast_print(Alloc *a, TokenFileSet *fset, Any x)` – Print prints x to standard output, skipping nil fields.
- `AstCommentMap ast_new_comment_map(Alloc *a, TokenFileSet *fset, AstNode node, Slice comments)` – NewCommentMap creates a new comment map by associating comment groups of the comments list with the nodes of the AST specified by node.
- `AstNode ast_comment_map_update(AstCommentMap cmap, Alloc *a, AstNode old, AstNode new_node)` – Update replaces an old node in the comment map with the new node and returns the new node.
- `AstCommentMap ast_comment_map_filter(AstCommentMap cmap, Alloc *a, AstNode node)` – Filter returns a new comment map consisting of only those entries of cmap for which a corresponding node exists in the AST specified by node.
- `Slice ast_comment_map_comments(AstCommentMap cmap, Alloc *a)` – Comments returns the list of comment groups in the comment map.
- `Str ast_comment_map_string(AstCommentMap cmap, Alloc *a)` – CommentMap.String: the map, one node per line, for debugging.

## burrow/go/constant.h

The sentences taken from Go are reproduced from the doc comments of Go's `go/constant` package, Copyright 2009 The Go Authors, BSD-3-Clause.

- `Str constant_kind_string(ConstantKind i, Alloc *a)` – Kind.String: "Unknown", "Bool" and so on, and "Kind(n)" for a value that is none of them.
- `ConstantKind constant_value_kind(ConstantValue x)` – Kind returns the value kind.
- `Str constant_value_string(ConstantValue x, Alloc *a)` – String returns a short, quoted (human-readable) form of the value.
- `Str constant_value_exact_string(ConstantValue x, Alloc *a)` – ExactString returns an exact, quoted (human-readable) form of the value.
- `extern const Type *const TYPE_CONSTANT_VALUE`
- `ConstantValue constant_make_unknown(void)` – MakeUnknown returns the Unknown value.
- `ConstantValue constant_make_bool(bool b)` – MakeBool returns the Bool value for b.
- `ConstantValue constant_make_int64(int64_t x)` – MakeInt64 returns the Int value for x.
- `ConstantValue constant_make_string(Alloc *a, Str s)` – MakeString returns the String value for s.
- `ConstantValue constant_make_uint64(Alloc *a, uint64_t x)` – MakeUint64 returns the Int value for x.
- `ConstantValue constant_make_float64(Alloc *a, double x)` – MakeFloat64 returns the Float value for x.
- `ConstantValue constant_make_from_literal(Alloc *a, Str lit, Token tok, Uint zero)` – MakeFromLiteral returns the corresponding integer, floating-point, imaginary, character, or string value for a Go literal string.
- `ConstantValue constant_make(Alloc *a, Any x)` – Make returns the Value for x.
- `ConstantValue constant_make_imag(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x)` – MakeImag returns the Complex value x\*i; x must be Int, Float, or Unknown.
- `ConstantValue constant_make_from_bytes(Alloc *a, Slice bytes)` – MakeFromBytes returns the Int value given the bytes of its little-endian binary representation.
- `bool constant_bool_val(ConstantValue x)` – BoolVal returns the Go boolean value of x, which must be a Bool or an Unknown.
- `Str constant_string_val(ConstantValue x)` – StringVal returns the Go string value of x, which must be a String or an Unknown.
- `int64_t constant_int64_val(ConstantValue x, bool *exact)` – Int64Val returns the Go int64 value of x and whether the result is exact; x must be an Int or an Unknown.
- `uint64_t constant_uint64_val(ConstantValue x, bool *exact)` – Uint64Val returns the Go uint64 value of x and whether the result is exact; x must be an Int or an Unknown.
- `float constant_float32_val(ConstantValue x, bool *exact)` – Float32Val is like Float64Val but for float32 instead of float64.
- `double constant_float64_val(ConstantValue x, bool *exact)` – Float64Val returns the nearest Go float64 value of x and whether the result is exact; x must be numeric or an Unknown, but not Complex.
- `Any constant_val(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x)` – Val returns the underlying value for a given constant.
- `int64_t constant_string_len(ConstantValue x)` – StringLen returns the length of x if x is a String.
- `Int constant_bit_len(ConstantValue x)` – BitLen returns the number of bits required to represent the absolute value x in binary representation; x must be an Int or an Unknown.
- `Int constant_sign(ConstantValue x)` – Sign returns -1, 0, or 1 depending on whether x \< 0, x == 0, or x \> 0; x must be numeric or Unknown.
- `Slice constant_bytes(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x)` – Bytes returns the bytes for the absolute value of x in little- endian binary representation; x must be an Int.
- `ConstantValue constant_num(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x)` – Num returns the numerator of x; x must be Int, Float, or Unknown.
- `ConstantValue constant_denom(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x)` – Denom returns the denominator of x; x must be Int, Float, or Unknown.
- `ConstantValue constant_real(ConstantValue x)` – Real returns the real part of x, which must be a numeric or unknown value.
- `ConstantValue constant_imag(ConstantValue x)` – Imag returns the imaginary part of x, which must be a numeric or unknown value.
- `ConstantValue constant_to_int(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x)` – ToInt converts x to an Int value if x is representable as an Int.
- `ConstantValue constant_to_float(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x)` – ToFloat converts x to a Float value if x is representable as a Float.
- `ConstantValue constant_to_complex(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x)` – ToComplex converts x to a Complex value if x is representable as a Complex.
- `ConstantValue constant_unary_op(Alloc *a, Token op, ConstantValue y, Uint prec)` – UnaryOp returns the result of the unary expression op y.
- `ConstantValue constant_binary_op(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x, Token op, ConstantValue y)` – BinaryOp returns the result of the binary expression x op y.
- `ConstantValue constant_shift(Alloc *a, ConstantValue x, Token op, Uint s)` – Shift returns the result of the shift expression x op s with op == token.SHL or token.SHR (\<\< or \>\>).
- `bool constant_compare(ConstantValue x, Token op, ConstantValue y)` – Compare returns the result of the comparison x op y.

- `bool token_pos_is_valid(TokenPos p)` – IsValid reports whether the position is valid.
- `Str token_file_string(TokenFile *f, Alloc *a)` – String returns a brief description of the File.
- `Str token_file_name(TokenFile *f)` – Name returns the file name of file f as registered with AddFile.
- `Int token_file_base(TokenFile *f)` – Base returns the base offset of file f as registered with AddFile.
- `Int token_file_size(TokenFile *f)` – Size returns the size of file f as registered with AddFile.
- `TokenPos token_file_end(TokenFile *f)` – End returns the end position of file f as registered with AddFile.
- `Int token_file_line_count(TokenFile *f)` – LineCount returns the number of lines in file f.
- `void token_file_add_line(TokenFile *f, Int offset)` – AddLine adds the line offset for a new line.
- `void token_file_merge_line(TokenFile *f, Int line)` – MergeLine merges a line with the following line.
- `Slice token_file_lines(TokenFile *f)` – Lines returns the effective line offset table of the form described by File.SetLines.
- `bool token_file_set_lines(TokenFile *f, Slice lines)` – SetLines sets the line offsets for a file and reports whether it succeeded.
- `void token_file_set_lines_for_content(TokenFile *f, Slice content)` – SetLinesForContent sets the line offsets for the given file content.
- `TokenPos token_file_line_start(TokenFile *f, Int line)` – LineStart returns the Pos value of the start of the specified line.
- `void token_file_add_line_info(TokenFile *f, Int offset, Str filename, Int line)` – AddLineInfo is like File.AddLineColumnInfo with a column = 1 argument.
- `void token_file_add_line_column_info(TokenFile *f, Int offset, Str filename, Int line, Int column)` – AddLineColumnInfo adds alternative file, line, and column number information for a given file offset.
- `TokenPos token_file_pos(TokenFile *f, Int offset)` – Pos returns the Pos value for the given file offset.
- `Int token_file_offset(TokenFile *f, TokenPos p)` – Offset returns the offset for the given file position p.
- `Int token_file_line(TokenFile *f, TokenPos p)` – Line returns the line number for the given file position p; p must be a Pos value in that file or NoPos.
- `TokenPosition token_file_position_for(TokenFile *f, TokenPos p, bool adjusted)` – PositionFor returns the Position value for the given file position p.
- `TokenPosition token_file_position(TokenFile *f, TokenPos p)` – Position returns the Position value for the given file position p.
- `TokenFileSet *token_new_file_set(Alloc *a)` – NewFileSet creates a new file set.
- `void token_file_set_free(TokenFileSet *s)` – Gives back s, the files it made and their line tables.
- `Int token_file_set_base(TokenFileSet *s)` – Base returns the minimum base offset that must be provided to FileSet.AddFile when adding the next file.
- `TokenFile *token_file_set_add_file(TokenFileSet *s, Str filename, Int base, Int size)` – AddFile adds a new file with a given filename, base offset, and file size to the file set s and returns the file.
- `void token_file_set_add_existing_files(TokenFileSet *s, TokenFile *const *files, Int n)` – AddExistingFiles adds the specified files to the FileSet if they are not already present.
- `void token_file_set_remove_file(TokenFileSet *s, TokenFile *file)` – RemoveFile removes a file from the FileSet so that subsequent queries for its Pos interval yield a negative result.
- `void token_file_set_iterate(TokenFileSet *s, TokenFileFunc yield)` – Iterate calls yield for the files in the file set in ascending Base order until yield returns false.
- `TokenFile *token_file_set_file(TokenFileSet *s, TokenPos p)` – File returns the file that contains the position p.
- `TokenPosition token_file_set_position_for(TokenFileSet *s, TokenPos p, bool adjusted)` – PositionFor converts a Pos p in the fileset into a Position value.
- `TokenPosition token_file_set_position(TokenFileSet *s, TokenPos p)` – Position converts a Pos p in the fileset into a Position value.
- `Error token_file_set_read(TokenFileSet *s, TokenCodecFunc decode)` – Read calls decode to deserialize a file set into s; s must not be nil.
- `Error token_file_set_write(TokenFileSet *s, TokenCodecFunc encode)` – Write calls encode to serialize the file set s.

## burrow/go/types.h

The sentences taken from Go are reproduced from the doc comments of Go's `go/types` package, Copyright 2009 The Go Authors, BSD-3-Clause.

- `#define types_type(x)` – A pointer to any of the types above as a TypesType, checked at compile time.
- `TypesBasic *types_as_basic(TypesType t)` – t as the type its kind says it is, or NULL if t is NULL or another kind.
- `TypesPointer *types_as_pointer(TypesType t)`
- `TypesArray *types_as_array(TypesType t)`
- `TypesSlice *types_as_slice(TypesType t)`
- `TypesStruct *types_as_struct(TypesType t)`
- `TypesTuple *types_as_tuple(TypesType t)`
- `TypesSignature *types_as_signature(TypesType t)`
- `TypesInterface *types_as_interface(TypesType t)`
- `TypesMap *types_as_map(TypesType t)`
- `TypesChan *types_as_chan(TypesType t)`
- `TypesNamed *types_as_named(TypesType t)`
- `TypesTypeParam *types_as_type_param(TypesType t)`
- `TypesUnion *types_as_union(TypesType t)`
- `TypesAlias *types_as_alias(TypesType t)`
- `TypesType types_type_underlying(TypesType t)` – Underlying returns the underlying type of a type.
- `TypesQualifier types_relative_to(TypesPackage *pkg)` – RelativeTo returns a Qualifier that fully qualifies members of all packages other than pkg.
- `Str types_type_string(Alloc *a, TypesType t, TypesQualifier qf)` – TypeString returns the string representation of typ.
- `void types_write_type(BytesBuffer *buf, TypesType t, TypesQualifier qf)` – WriteType writes the string representation of typ to buf.
- `Str types_expr_string(Alloc *a, AstExpr x)` – ExprString returns the (possibly shortened) string representation for x.
- `void types_write_expr(BytesBuffer *buf, AstExpr x)` – WriteExpr writes the (possibly shortened) string representation for x to buf.
- `extern TypesBasic *const types_typ[26]` – Typ contains the predeclared \*Basic types indexed by their corresponding BasicKind.
- `TypesBasicKind types_basic_kind(TypesBasic *b)` – Kind returns the kind of basic type b.
- `TypesBasicInfo types_basic_info(TypesBasic *b)` – Info returns information about properties of basic type b.
- `Str types_basic_name(TypesBasic *b)` – Name returns the name of basic type b.
- `TypesPointer *types_new_pointer(Alloc *a, TypesType elem)` – NewPointer returns a new pointer type for the given element (base) type.
- `TypesType types_pointer_elem(TypesPointer *p)` – Elem returns the element type for the given pointer p.
- `TypesArray *types_new_array(Alloc *a, TypesType elem, int64_t len)` – NewArray returns a new array type for the given element type and length.
- `int64_t types_array_len(TypesArray *t)` – Len returns the length of array a.
- `TypesType types_array_elem(TypesArray *t)` – Elem returns element type of array a.
- `TypesSlice *types_new_slice(Alloc *a, TypesType elem)` – NewSlice returns a new slice type for the given element type.
- `TypesType types_slice_elem(TypesSlice *s)` – Elem returns the element type of slice s.
- `TypesMap *types_new_map(Alloc *a, TypesType key, TypesType elem)` – NewMap returns a new map for the given key and element types.
- `TypesType types_map_key(TypesMap *m)` – Key returns the key type of map m.
- `TypesType types_map_elem(TypesMap *m)` – Elem returns the element type of map m.
- `TypesChan *types_new_chan(Alloc *a, TypesChanDir dir, TypesType elem)` – NewChan returns a new channel type for the given direction and element type.
- `TypesChanDir types_chan_dir(TypesChan *c)` – Dir returns the direction of channel c.
- `TypesType types_chan_elem(TypesChan *c)` – Elem returns the element type of channel c.
- `TypesStruct *types_new_struct(Alloc *a, Slice fields, Slice tags)` – NewStruct returns a new struct with the given fields and corresponding field tags.
- `Int types_struct_num_fields(TypesStruct *s)` – NumFields returns the number of fields in the struct (including blank and embedded fields).
- `TypesVar *types_struct_field(TypesStruct *s, Int i)` – Field returns the i'th field for 0 \<= i \< NumFields().
- `Str types_struct_tag(TypesStruct *s, Int i)` – Tag returns the i'th field tag for 0 \<= i \< NumFields().
- `TypesTuple *types_new_tuple(Alloc *a, Slice vars)` – NewTuple returns a new tuple for the given variables.
- `Int types_tuple_len(TypesTuple *t)` – Len returns the number variables of tuple t.
- `TypesVar *types_tuple_at(TypesTuple *t, Int i)` – At returns the i'th variable of tuple t.
- `TypesSignature *types_new_signature_type(Alloc *a, TypesVar *recv, Slice recv_type_params, Slice type_params, TypesTuple *params, TypesTuple *results, bool variadic)` – NewSignatureType creates a new function type for the given receiver, receiver type parameters, type parameters, parameters, and results.
- `TypesSignature *types_new_signature(Alloc *a, TypesVar *recv, TypesTuple *params, TypesTuple *results, bool variadic)` – NewSignature returns a new function type for the given receiver, parameters, and results, either of which may be nil.
- `TypesVar *types_signature_recv(TypesSignature *s)` – Recv returns the receiver of signature s (if a method), or nil if a function.
- `TypesTypeParamList *types_signature_type_params(TypesSignature *s)` – TypeParams returns the type parameters of signature s, or nil.
- `TypesTypeParamList *types_signature_recv_type_params(TypesSignature *s)` – RecvTypeParams returns the receiver type parameters of signature s, or nil.
- `TypesTuple *types_signature_params(TypesSignature *s)` – Params returns the parameters of signature s, or nil.
- `TypesTuple *types_signature_results(TypesSignature *s)` – Results returns the results of signature s, or nil.
- `bool types_signature_variadic(TypesSignature *s)` – Variadic reports whether the signature s is variadic.
- `void types_write_signature(BytesBuffer *buf, TypesSignature *sig, TypesQualifier qf)` – WriteSignature writes the representation of the signature sig to buf, without a leading "func" keyword.
- `TypesInterface *types_new_interface_type(Alloc *a, Slice methods, Slice embeddeds)` – NewInterfaceType returns a new interface for the given methods and embedded types.
- `TypesInterface *types_new_interface(Alloc *a, Slice methods, Slice embeddeds)` – NewInterface returns a new interface for the given methods and embedded types.
- `void types_interface_mark_implicit(TypesInterface *t)` – MarkImplicit marks the interface t as implicit, meaning this interface corresponds to a constraint literal such as ~T or A|B without explicit interface embedding.
- `Int types_interface_num_explicit_methods(TypesInterface *t)` – NumExplicitMethods returns the number of explicitly declared methods of interface t.
- `TypesFunc *types_interface_explicit_method(TypesInterface *t, Int i)` – ExplicitMethod returns the i'th explicitly declared method of interface t for 0 \<= i \< t.NumExplicitMethods().
- `Int types_interface_num_embeddeds(TypesInterface *t)` – NumEmbeddeds returns the number of embedded types in interface t.
- `TypesNamed *types_interface_embedded(TypesInterface *t, Int i)` – Embedded returns the i'th embedded defined (\*\[Named\]) type of interface t for 0 \<= i \< t.NumEmbeddeds().
- `TypesType types_interface_embedded_type(TypesInterface *t, Int i)` – EmbeddedType returns the i'th embedded type of interface t for 0 \<= i \< t.NumEmbeddeds().
- `Int types_interface_num_methods(TypesInterface *t)` – NumMethods returns the total number of methods of interface t.
- `TypesFunc *types_interface_method(TypesInterface *t, Int i)` – Method returns the i'th method of interface t for 0 \<= i \< t.NumMethods().
- `bool types_interface_empty(TypesInterface *t)` – Empty reports whether t is the empty interface.
- `bool types_interface_is_comparable(TypesInterface *t)` – IsComparable reports whether each type in interface t's type set is comparable.
- `bool types_interface_is_method_set(TypesInterface *t)` – IsMethodSet reports whether the interface t is fully described by its method set.
- `bool types_interface_is_implicit(TypesInterface *t)` – IsImplicit reports whether the interface t is a wrapper for a type set literal.
- `TypesInterface *types_interface_complete(TypesInterface *t)` – Complete computes the interface's type set.
- `TypesTerm *types_new_term(Alloc *a, bool tilde, TypesType typ)` – NewTerm returns a new union term.
- `bool types_term_tilde(TypesTerm *t)`
- `TypesType types_term_type(TypesTerm *t)`
- `Str types_term_string(Alloc *a, TypesTerm *t)` – Term.String: "~int", "int", and for the special terms of a type set, "∅" for a NULL term and "𝓤" for one with no type.
- `TypesUnion *types_new_union(Alloc *a, Slice terms)` – NewUnion returns a new Union type with the given terms.
- `Int types_union_len(TypesUnion *u)`
- `TypesTerm *types_union_term(TypesUnion *u, Int i)`
- `TypesTypeParam *types_new_type_param(Alloc *a, TypesTypeName *obj, TypesType constraint)` – NewTypeParam returns a new TypeParam.
- `TypesTypeName *types_type_param_obj(TypesTypeParam *t)` – Obj returns the type name for the type parameter t.
- `Int types_type_param_index(TypesTypeParam *t)` – Index returns the index of the type param within its param list, or -1 if the type parameter has not yet been bound to a type.
- `TypesType types_type_param_constraint(TypesTypeParam *t)` – Constraint returns the type constraint specified for t.
- `void types_type_param_set_constraint(TypesTypeParam *t, TypesType bound)` – SetConstraint sets the type constraint for t.
- `Int types_type_param_list_len(TypesTypeParamList *l)` – Len returns the number of type parameters in the list.
- `TypesTypeParam *types_type_param_list_at(TypesTypeParamList *l, Int i)` – At returns the i'th type parameter in the list.
- `Str types_type_param_list_string(Alloc *a, TypesTypeParamList *l)`
- `Int types_type_list_len(TypesTypeList *l)` – Len returns the number of types in the list.
- `TypesType types_type_list_at(TypesTypeList *l, Int i)` – At returns the i'th type in the list.
- `Str types_type_list_string(Alloc *a, TypesTypeList *l)`
- `TypesNamed *types_new_named(Alloc *a, TypesTypeName *obj, TypesType underlying, Slice methods)` – NewNamed returns a new named type for the given type name, underlying type, and associated methods.
- `TypesTypeName *types_named_obj(TypesNamed *t)` – Obj returns the type name for the declaration defining the named type t.
- `TypesNamed *types_named_origin(TypesNamed *t)` – Origin returns the generic type from which the named type t is instantiated.
- `TypesTypeParamList *types_named_type_params(TypesNamed *t)` – TypeParams returns the type parameters of the named type t, or nil.
- `void types_named_set_type_params(TypesNamed *t, Slice tparams)` – SetTypeParams sets the type parameters of the named type t.
- `TypesTypeList *types_named_type_args(TypesNamed *t)` – TypeArgs returns the type arguments used to instantiate the named type t.
- `Int types_named_num_methods(TypesNamed *t)` – NumMethods returns the number of explicit methods defined for t.
- `TypesFunc *types_named_method(TypesNamed *t, Int i)` – Method returns the i'th method of named type t for 0 \<= i \< t.NumMethods().
- `void types_named_set_underlying(TypesNamed *t, TypesType u)` – SetUnderlying sets the underlying type and marks t as complete.
- `void types_named_add_method(TypesNamed *t, TypesFunc *m)` – AddMethod adds method m unless it is already in the method list.
- `TypesAlias *types_new_alias(Alloc *a, TypesTypeName *obj, TypesType rhs)` – NewAlias creates a new Alias type with the given type name and rhs.
- `TypesTypeName *types_alias_obj(TypesAlias *a)` – Obj returns the type name for the declaration defining the alias type a.
- `TypesAlias *types_alias_origin(TypesAlias *a)` – Origin returns the generic Alias type of which a is an instance.
- `TypesTypeParamList *types_alias_type_params(TypesAlias *a)` – TypeParams returns the type parameters of the alias type a, or nil.
- `void types_alias_set_type_params(TypesAlias *a, Slice tparams)` – SetTypeParams sets the type parameters of the alias type a.
- `TypesTypeList *types_alias_type_args(TypesAlias *a)` – TypeArgs returns the type arguments used to instantiate the Alias type.
- `TypesType types_alias_rhs(TypesAlias *a)` – Rhs returns the type R on the right-hand side of an alias declaration "type A = R", which may be another alias.
- `TypesType types_unalias(TypesType t)` – Unalias returns t if it is not an alias type; otherwise it follows t's alias chain until it reaches a non-alias type which is then returned.
- `#define types_object(x)` – A pointer to any of the objects as a TypesObject, checked at compile time.
- `TypesPkgName *types_as_pkg_name(TypesObject obj)` – obj as the object its kind says it is, or NULL.
- `TypesConst *types_as_const(TypesObject obj)`
- `TypesTypeName *types_as_type_name(TypesObject obj)`
- `TypesVar *types_as_var(TypesObject obj)`
- `TypesFunc *types_as_func(TypesObject obj)`
- `TypesLabel *types_as_label(TypesObject obj)`
- `TypesBuiltin *types_as_builtin(TypesObject obj)`
- `TypesNil *types_as_nil(TypesObject obj)`
- `TypesScope *types_object_parent(TypesObject obj)` – Object.Parent: the scope obj is declared in, NULL for methods and fields.
- `TokenPos types_object_pos(TypesObject obj)` – Object.Pos: where the identifier that declares obj is.
- `TypesPackage *types_object_pkg(TypesObject obj)` – Object.Pkg: the package obj belongs to, NULL for labels and for the objects of the universe.
- `Str types_object_name(TypesObject obj)`
- `TypesType types_object_type(TypesObject obj)`
- `bool types_object_exported(TypesObject obj)` – Object.Exported: the name starts with a capital letter.
- `Str types_object_id(Alloc *a, TypesObject obj)` – Object.Id: types_id of obj's package and name.
- `Str types_id(Alloc *a, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name)` – Id returns name if it is exported, otherwise it returns the name qualified with the package path.
- `Str types_object_string(Alloc *a, TypesObject obj, TypesQualifier qf)` – ObjectString returns the string form of obj.
- `TypesPkgName *types_new_pkg_name(Alloc *a, TokenPos pos, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name, TypesPackage *imported)` – NewPkgName returns a new PkgName object representing an imported package.
- `TypesPackage *types_pkg_name_imported(TypesPkgName *obj)` – Imported returns the package that was imported.
- `TypesConst *types_new_const(Alloc *a, TokenPos pos, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name, TypesType typ, ConstantValue val)` – NewConst returns a new constant with value val.
- `ConstantValue types_const_val(TypesConst *obj)` – Val returns the constant's value.
- `TypesTypeName *types_new_type_name(Alloc *a, TokenPos pos, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name, TypesType typ)` – NewTypeName returns a new type name denoting the given typ.
- `bool types_type_name_is_alias(TypesTypeName *obj)` – IsAlias reports whether obj is an alias name for a type.
- `Str types_var_kind_string(Alloc *a, TypesVarKind kind)` – VarKind.String: "PackageVar" and so on, and "VarKind(n)" for any other n.
- `TypesVar *types_new_var(Alloc *a, TokenPos pos, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name, TypesType typ)` – NewVar returns a new variable.
- `TypesVar *types_new_param(Alloc *a, TokenPos pos, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name, TypesType typ)` – NewParam returns a new variable representing a function parameter.
- `TypesVar *types_new_field(Alloc *a, TokenPos pos, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name, TypesType typ, bool embedded)` – NewField returns a new variable representing a struct field.
- `TypesVarKind types_var_kind(TypesVar *v)` – Kind reports what kind of variable v is.
- `void types_var_set_kind(TypesVar *v, TypesVarKind kind)` – SetKind sets the kind of the variable.
- `bool types_var_anonymous(TypesVar *v)` – Anonymous reports whether the variable is an embedded field.
- `bool types_var_embedded(TypesVar *v)` – Embedded reports whether the variable is an embedded field.
- `bool types_var_is_field(TypesVar *v)` – IsField reports whether the variable is a struct field.
- `TypesVar *types_var_origin(TypesVar *v)` – Origin returns the canonical Var for its receiver, i.e.
- `TypesFunc *types_new_func(Alloc *a, TokenPos pos, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name, TypesSignature *sig)` – NewFunc returns a new function with the given signature, representing the function's type.
- `TypesSignature *types_func_signature(TypesFunc *obj)` – Signature returns the signature (type) of the function or method.
- `Str types_func_full_name(Alloc *a, TypesFunc *obj)` – FullName returns the package- or receiver-type-qualified name of function or method obj.
- `TypesScope *types_func_scope(TypesFunc *obj)` – Scope returns the scope of the function's body block.
- `TypesFunc *types_func_origin(TypesFunc *obj)` – Origin returns the canonical Func for its receiver, i.e.
- `TypesLabel *types_new_label(Alloc *a, TokenPos pos, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name)` – NewLabel returns a new label.
- `TypesScope *types_new_scope(Alloc *a, TypesScope *parent, TokenPos pos, TokenPos end, Str comment)` – NewScope returns a new, empty scope contained in the given parent scope, if any.
- `TypesScope *types_scope_parent(TypesScope *s)` – Parent returns the scope's containing (parent) scope.
- `Int types_scope_len(TypesScope *s)` – Len returns the number of scope elements.
- `Slice types_scope_names(Alloc *a, TypesScope *s)` – Names returns the scope's element names in sorted order.
- `Int types_scope_num_children(TypesScope *s)` – NumChildren returns the number of scopes nested in s.
- `TypesScope *types_scope_child(TypesScope *s, Int i)` – Child returns the i'th child scope for 0 \<= i \< NumChildren().
- `TypesObject types_scope_lookup(TypesScope *s, Str name)` – Lookup returns the object in scope s with the given name if such an object exists; otherwise the result is nil.
- `TypesObject types_scope_lookup_parent(TypesScope *s, Str name, TokenPos pos, TypesScope **scope)` – LookupParent follows the parent chain of scopes starting with s until it finds a scope where Lookup(name) returns a non-nil object, and then returns that scope and object.
- `TypesObject types_scope_insert(TypesScope *s, TypesObject obj)` – Insert attempts to insert an object obj into scope s.
- `TokenPos types_scope_pos(TypesScope *s)` – Pos and End describe the scope's source code extent \[pos, end).
- `TokenPos types_scope_end(TypesScope *s)`
- `bool types_scope_contains(TypesScope *s, TokenPos pos)` – Contains reports whether pos is within the scope's extent.
- `TypesScope *types_scope_innermost(TypesScope *s, TokenPos pos)` – Innermost returns the innermost (child) scope containing pos.
- `void types_scope_write_to(TypesScope *s, IoWriter w, Int n, bool recurse)` – WriteTo writes a string representation of the scope to w, with the scope elements sorted by name.
- `Str types_scope_string(Alloc *a, TypesScope *s)` – String returns a string representation of the scope, for debugging.
- `TypesPackage *types_new_package(Alloc *a, Str path, Str name)` – NewPackage returns a new Package for the given package path and name.
- `Str types_package_path(TypesPackage *pkg)` – Path returns the package path.
- `Str types_package_name(TypesPackage *pkg)` – Name returns the package name.
- `void types_package_set_name(TypesPackage *pkg, Str name)` – SetName sets the package name.
- `Str types_package_go_version(TypesPackage *pkg)` – GoVersion returns the minimum Go version required by this package.
- `TypesScope *types_package_scope(TypesPackage *pkg)` – Scope returns the (complete or incomplete) package scope holding the objects declared at package level (TypeNames, Consts, Vars, and Funcs).
- `bool types_package_complete(TypesPackage *pkg)` – A package is complete if its scope contains (at least) all exported objects; otherwise it is incomplete.
- `void types_package_mark_complete(TypesPackage *pkg)` – MarkComplete marks a package as complete.
- `Slice types_package_imports(TypesPackage *pkg)` – Imports returns the list of packages directly imported by pkg; the list is in source order.
- `void types_package_set_imports(TypesPackage *pkg, Slice list)` – SetImports sets the list of explicitly imported packages to list.
- `Str types_package_string(Alloc *a, TypesPackage *pkg)` – Package.String: package name ("path").
- `TypesScope *types_universe(void)` – The Universe scope contains all predeclared objects of Go.
- `TypesPackage *types_unsafe(void)` – The Unsafe package is the package returned by an importer for the import path "unsafe".
- `void types_def_predeclared_test_funcs(void)` – DefPredeclaredTestFuncs defines the assert and trace built-ins.
- `bool types_identical(TypesType x, TypesType y)` – Identical reports whether x and y are identical types.
- `bool types_identical_ignore_tags(TypesType x, TypesType y)` – IdenticalIgnoreTags reports whether x and y are identical types if tags are ignored.
- `bool types_comparable(TypesType t)` – Comparable reports whether values of type T are comparable.
- `bool types_is_interface(TypesType t)` – IsInterface reports whether t is an interface type.
- `TypesType types_default(TypesType t)` – Default returns the default "typed" type for an "untyped" type; it returns the incoming type for all other types.
- `void types_hasher_hash(TypesHasher hr, MaphashHash *h, TypesType t)` – Hasher.Hash: writes t to h.
- `bool types_hasher_equal(TypesHasher hr, TypesType x, TypesType y)` – Hasher.Equal: types_identical(x, y).
- `void types_hasher_ignore_tags_hash(TypesHasherIgnoreTags hr, MaphashHash *h, TypesType t)`
- `bool types_hasher_ignore_tags_equal(TypesHasherIgnoreTags hr, TypesType x, TypesType y)`
- `MaphashHasher types_hasher_as_hasher(TypesHasher hr)` – Each as a maphash.Hasher\[Type\]: the values handed to it point at a TypesType.
- `MaphashHasher types_hasher_ignore_tags_as_hasher(TypesHasherIgnoreTags hr)`
- `TypesSelection *types_lookup_selection(Alloc *a, TypesType T, bool addressable, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name)` – LookupSelection selects the field or method whose ID is Id(pkg, name), on a value of type T. If addressable is set, T is the type of an addressable variable (this matters only for method lookups).
- `TypesObject types_lookup_field_or_method(Alloc *a, TypesType T, bool addressable, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name, Slice *index, bool *indirect)` – LookupFieldOrMethod looks up a field or method with given package and name in T and returns the corresponding \*Var or \*Func, an index sequence, and a bool indicating if there were any pointer indirections on the path to the field or method.
- `TypesFunc *types_missing_method(TypesType V, TypesInterface *T, bool is_static, bool *wrong_type)` – MissingMethod returns (nil, false) if V implements T, otherwise it returns a missing method required by T and whether it is missing or just has the wrong type: either a pointer receiver or wrong signature.
- `TypesSelectionKind types_selection_kind(TypesSelection *s)` – Kind returns the selection kind.
- `TypesType types_selection_recv(TypesSelection *s)` – Recv returns the type of x in x.f.
- `TypesObject types_selection_obj(TypesSelection *s)` – Obj returns the object denoted by x.f; a \*Var for a field selection, and a \*Func in all other cases.
- `TypesType types_selection_type(Alloc *a, TypesSelection *s)` – Type returns the type of x.f, which may be different from the type of f.
- `Slice types_selection_index(TypesSelection *s)` – Index describes the path from x to f in x.f.
- `bool types_selection_indirect(TypesSelection *s)` – Indirect reports whether any pointer indirection was required to get from x to f in x.f.
- `void types_write_selection(BytesBuffer *buf, TypesSelection *s, TypesQualifier qf)` – WriteSelection and SelectionString: s as text, such as "field (T) f int".
- `Str types_selection_string(Alloc *a, TypesSelection *s, TypesQualifier qf)` – SelectionString returns the string form of s.
- `TypesMethodSet *types_new_method_set(Alloc *a, TypesType T)` – NewMethodSet returns the method set for the given type T. It always returns a non-nil method set, even if it is empty.
- `Int types_method_set_len(TypesMethodSet *s)` – Len returns the number of methods in s.
- `TypesSelection *types_method_set_at(TypesMethodSet *s, Int i)` – At returns the i'th method in s for 0 \<= i \< s.Len().
- `TypesSelection *types_method_set_lookup(TypesMethodSet *s, TypesPackage *pkg, Str name)` – Lookup returns the method with matching package and name, or nil if not found.
- `Str types_method_set_string(Alloc *a, TypesMethodSet *s)`
- `int64_t types_sizes_alignof(TypesSizes s, TypesType T)` – Alignof returns the alignment of a variable of type T. Alignof must implement the alignment guarantees required by the spec.
- `Slice types_sizes_offsetsof(TypesSizes s, Alloc *a, Slice fields)` – Offsetsof returns the offsets of the given struct fields, in bytes.
- `int64_t types_sizes_sizeof(TypesSizes s, TypesType T)` – Sizeof returns the size of a variable of type T. Sizeof must implement the size guarantees required by the spec.
- `int64_t types_std_sizes_alignof(TypesStdSizes *s, TypesType T)`
- `Slice types_std_sizes_offsetsof(TypesStdSizes *s, Alloc *a, Slice fields)`
- `int64_t types_std_sizes_sizeof(TypesStdSizes *s, TypesType T)`
- `TypesSizes types_std_sizes_as_sizes(TypesStdSizes *s)`
- `TypesSizes types_sizes_for(Str compiler, Str arch)` – SizesFor returns the Sizes used by a compiler for an architecture.
- `TypesContext *types_new_context(Alloc *a)` – NewContext creates a new Context.
- `TypesType types_instantiate(Alloc *a, TypesContext *ctxt, TypesType orig, Slice targs, bool validate, Error *err)` – Instantiate instantiates the type orig with the given type arguments targs.
- `bool types_assertable_to(TypesInterface *V, TypesType T)` – AssertableTo reports whether a value of type V can be asserted to have type T.
- `bool types_assignable_to(TypesType V, TypesType T)` – AssignableTo reports whether a value of type V is assignable to a variable of type T.
- `bool types_convertible_to(TypesType V, TypesType T)` – ConvertibleTo reports whether a value of type V is convertible to a value of type T.
- `bool types_implements(TypesType V, TypesInterface *T)` – Implements reports whether type V implements interface T.
- `bool types_satisfies(TypesType V, TypesInterface *T)` – Satisfies reports whether type V satisfies the constraint T.
- `extern const Type *const TYPE_TYPES_ERROR`
- `Str types_error_error(const TypesError *e, Alloc *a)` – Error returns an error string formatted as follows: filename:line:column: message
- `Error types_error_as_error(const TypesError *e, Alloc *a)` – The Error for e, with msg copied into a.
- `extern const Type *const TYPE_TYPES_ARGUMENT_ERROR`
- `Str types_argument_error_error(const TypesArgumentError *e, Alloc *a)` – The text, made in a.
- `Error types_argument_error_unwrap(const TypesArgumentError *e)` – e-\>err.
- `Error types_argument_error_as_error(const TypesArgumentError *e, Alloc *a)` – The Error for e, in a.
- `extern const Type *const TYPE_TYPES_TYPE_AND_VALUE`
- `bool types_type_and_value_is_void(TypesTypeAndValue tv)` – IsVoid reports whether the corresponding expression is a function call without results.
- `bool types_type_and_value_is_type(TypesTypeAndValue tv)` – IsType reports whether the corresponding expression specifies a type.
- `bool types_type_and_value_is_builtin(TypesTypeAndValue tv)` – IsBuiltin reports whether the corresponding expression denotes a (possibly parenthesized) built-in function.
- `bool types_type_and_value_is_value(TypesTypeAndValue tv)` – IsValue reports whether the corresponding expression is a value.
- `bool types_type_and_value_is_nil(TypesTypeAndValue tv)` – IsNil reports whether the corresponding expression denotes the predeclared value nil.
- `bool types_type_and_value_addressable(TypesTypeAndValue tv)` – Addressable reports whether the corresponding expression is addressable (https://golang.org/ref/spec#Address_operators).
- `bool types_type_and_value_assignable(TypesTypeAndValue tv)` – Assignable reports whether the corresponding expression is assignable to (provided a value of the right type).
- `bool types_type_and_value_has_ok(TypesTypeAndValue tv)` – HasOk reports whether the corresponding expression may be used on the rhs of a comma-ok assignment.
- `extern const Type *const TYPE_TYPES_INSTANCE`
- `Str types_initializer_string(Alloc *a, const TypesInitializer *init)` – Initializer.String, such as "x, y = f()".
- `TypesInfo types_info_all(Alloc *a)` – Not in Go: an Info with every map made in a, for when you want all of it.
- `TypesType types_info_type_of(TypesInfo *info, AstExpr e)` – TypeOf returns the type of expression e, or nil if not found.
- `TypesObject types_info_object_of(TypesInfo *info, AstIdent *id)` – ObjectOf returns the object denoted by the specified id, or nil if not found.
- `TypesPkgName *types_info_pkg_name_of(TypesInfo *info, AstImportSpec *imp)` – PkgNameOf returns the local package name defined by the import, or nil if not found.
- `TypesChecker *types_new_checker(Alloc *a, TypesConfig *conf, TokenFileSet *fset, TypesPackage *pkg, TypesInfo *info)` – NewChecker returns a new Checker instance for a given package.
- `bool types_checker_files(TypesChecker *check, Slice files, Error *err)` – Files checks the provided files as part of the checker's package.
- `TypesPackage *types_config_check(TypesConfig *conf, Alloc *a, Str path, TokenFileSet *fset, Slice files, TypesInfo *info, Error *err)` – Check type-checks a package and returns the resulting package object and the first error if any.
- `bool types_check_expr(Alloc *a, TokenFileSet *fset, TypesPackage *pkg, TokenPos pos, AstExpr expr, TypesInfo *info, Error *err)` – CheckExpr type checks the expression expr as if it had appeared at position pos of package pkg.
- `TypesTypeAndValue types_eval(Alloc *a, TokenFileSet *fset, TypesPackage *pkg, TokenPos pos, Str expr, Error *err)` – Eval returns the type and, if constant, the value for the expression expr, evaluated at position pos of package pkg, which must have been derived from type-checking an AST with complete position information relative to the provided file set.

## burrow/hash/maphash.h

The sentences taken from Go are reproduced from the doc comments of Go's `hash/maphash` package, Copyright 2009 The Go Authors, BSD-3-Clause.

- `MaphashSeed maphash_make_seed(void)` – MakeSeed returns a new random seed.
- `uint64_t maphash_bytes(MaphashSeed seed, Slice b)` – Bytes returns the hash of b with the given seed.
- `uint64_t maphash_string(MaphashSeed seed, Str s)` – String returns the hash of s with the given seed.
- `uint64_t maphash_comparable(MaphashSeed seed, const Type *t, const void *v)` – Comparable returns the hash of comparable value v with the given seed such that Comparable(s, v1) == Comparable(s, v2) if v1 == v2.
- `void maphash_write_comparable(MaphashHash *h, const Type *t, const void *v)` – WriteComparable adds x to the data hashed by h.
- `Int maphash_hash_write(MaphashHash *h, Slice b, Error *err)` – Write adds b to the sequence of bytes hashed by h.
- `Int maphash_hash_write_string(MaphashHash *h, Str s, Error *err)` – WriteString adds the bytes of s to the sequence of bytes hashed by h.
- `Error maphash_hash_write_byte(MaphashHash *h, Byte c)` – WriteByte adds b to the sequence of bytes hashed by h.
- `MaphashSeed maphash_hash_seed(MaphashHash *h)` – Seed returns h's seed value.
- `void maphash_hash_set_seed(MaphashHash *h, MaphashSeed seed)` – SetSeed sets h to use seed, which must have been returned by MakeSeed or by another Hash.Seed method.
- `void maphash_hash_reset(MaphashHash *h)` – Reset discards all bytes added to h.
- `uint64_t maphash_hash_sum64(MaphashHash *h)` – Sum64 returns h's current 64-bit value, which depends on h's seed and the sequence of bytes added to h since the last call to Hash.Reset or Hash.SetSeed.
- `Slice maphash_hash_sum(MaphashHash *h, Alloc *a, Slice b)` – Sum appends the hash's current 64-bit value to b.
- `Int maphash_hash_size(const MaphashHash *h)` – Size returns h's hash value size, 8 bytes.
- `Int maphash_hash_block_size(const MaphashHash *h)` – BlockSize returns h's block size.
- `HashCloner maphash_hash_clone(MaphashHash *h, Alloc *a, Error *err)` – Clone implements hash.Cloner.
- `Hash maphash_hash_as_hash(MaphashHash *h)` – h as the hash interfaces, so it can go where a Hash, a HashHash64 or a HashCloner is wanted.
- `HashHash64 maphash_hash_as_hash64(MaphashHash *h)`
- `HashCloner maphash_hash_as_cloner(MaphashHash *h)`
- `void maphash_hasher_hash(MaphashHasher hr, MaphashHash *h, const void *v)`
- `bool maphash_hasher_equal(MaphashHasher hr, const void *x, const void *y)`
- `void maphash_comparable_hasher_hash(MaphashComparableHasher ch, MaphashHash *h, const void *v)` – maphash_write_comparable(h, ch.t, v).
- `bool maphash_comparable_hasher_equal(MaphashComparableHasher ch, const void *x, const void *y)` – type_equal(ch.t, x, y), which is Go's x == y.
- `MaphashHasher maphash_comparable_hasher_as_hasher(MaphashComparableHasher *ch)` – ch as a MaphashHasher.

The sentences taken from Go are reproduced from the doc comments of Go's `html/template` package, Copyright 2009 The Go Authors, BSD-3-Clause.

- `extern const Type *const TYPE_HTML_TEMPLATE_ERROR`
- `Str html_template_error_error(const HtmlTemplateError *e, Alloc *a)` – Error.Error: the message, made in a, as html/template:LOCATION: DESCRIPTION, where the location is the node's when there is one and the name and line when not.
- `Error html_template_error_as_error(Alloc *a, const HtmlTemplateError *e)` – An Error for e, with its message made and e copied into a.

- `Str html_template_name(const HtmlTemplate *t)` – Name returns the name of the template.
- `Slice html_template_templates(HtmlTemplate *t, Alloc *a)` – Templates returns a slice of the templates associated with t, including t itself.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_option(HtmlTemplate *t, Slice opt)` – Option sets options for the template.
- `Error html_template_execute(HtmlTemplate *t, IoWriter wr, Any data)` – Execute applies a parsed template to the specified data object, writing the output to wr.
- `Error html_template_execute_template(HtmlTemplate *t, IoWriter wr, Str name, Any data)` – ExecuteTemplate applies the template associated with t that has the given name to the specified data object and writes the output to wr.
- `Str html_template_defined_templates(HtmlTemplate *t, Alloc *a)` – DefinedTemplates returns a string listing the defined templates, prefixed by the string "; defined templates are: ".
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_parse(HtmlTemplate *t, Str text, Error *err)` – Parse parses text as a template body for t.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_add_parse_tree(HtmlTemplate *t, Str name, ParseTree *tree, Error *err)` – AddParseTree creates a new template with the name and parse tree and associates it with t.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_clone(HtmlTemplate *t, Alloc *a, Error *err)` – Clone returns a duplicate of the template, including all associated templates.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_funcs(HtmlTemplate *t, HtmlTemplateFuncMap funcs)` – Funcs adds the elements of the argument map to the template's function map.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_delims(HtmlTemplate *t, Str left, Str right)` – Delims sets the action delimiters to the specified strings, to be used in subsequent calls to Template.Parse, ParseFiles, or ParseGlob.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_lookup(HtmlTemplate *t, Str name)` – Lookup returns the template with the given name that is associated with t, or nil if there is no such template.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_must(HtmlTemplate *t, Error err)` – Must is a helper that wraps a call to a function returning (\*Template, error) and panics if the error is non-nil.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_parse_files(HtmlTemplate *t, Slice filenames, Error *err)` – ParseFiles parses the named files and associates the resulting templates with t.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_from_files(Alloc *a, Slice filenames, Error *err)` – ParseFiles creates a new Template and parses the template definitions from the named files.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_parse_glob(HtmlTemplate *t, Str pattern, Error *err)` – ParseGlob parses the template definitions in the files identified by the pattern and associates the resulting templates with t.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_from_glob(Alloc *a, Str pattern, Error *err)` – ParseGlob creates a new Template and parses the template definitions from the files identified by the pattern.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_parse_fs(HtmlTemplate *t, Fs fsys, Slice patterns, Error *err)` – ParseFS is like Template.ParseFiles or Template.ParseGlob but reads from the file system fs instead of the host operating system's file system.
- `HtmlTemplate *html_template_from_fs(Alloc *a, Fs fsys, Slice patterns, Error *err)` – ParseFS is like ParseFiles or ParseGlob but reads from the file system fs instead of the host operating system's file system.
- `void html_template_html_escape(IoWriter w, Slice b)` – HTMLEscape writes to w the escaped HTML equivalent of the plain text data b.
- `Str html_template_html_escape_string(Alloc *a, Str s)` – HTMLEscapeString returns the escaped HTML equivalent of the plain text data s.
- `Str html_template_html_escaper(Alloc *a, Slice args)` – HTMLEscaper returns the escaped HTML equivalent of the textual representation of its arguments.
- `void html_template_js_escape(IoWriter w, Slice b)` – JSEscape writes to w the escaped JavaScript equivalent of the plain text data b.
- `Str html_template_js_escape_string(Alloc *a, Str s)` – JSEscapeString returns the escaped JavaScript equivalent of the plain text data s.
- `Str html_template_js_escaper(Alloc *a, Slice args)` – JSEscaper returns the escaped JavaScript equivalent of the textual representation of its arguments.
- `Str html_template_url_query_escaper(Alloc *a, Slice args)` – URLQueryEscaper returns the escaped value of the textual representation of its arguments in a form suitable for embedding in a URL query.
- `bool html_template_is_true(Any val, bool *ok)` – IsTrue reports whether the value is 'true', in the sense of not the zero of its type, and whether the value has a meaningful truth value.

## burrow/image/color/palette.h

The sentences taken from Go are reproduced from the doc comments of Go's `image/color/palette` package, Copyright 2009 The Go Authors, BSD-3-Clause.

- `extern const ColorPalette palette_plan9` – Plan9 is a 256-color palette that partitions the 24-bit RGB space into 4×4×4 subdivision, with 4 shades in each subcube.
- `extern const ColorPalette palette_web_safe` – WebSafe is a 216-color palette that was popularized by early versions of Netscape Navigator.

## burrow/image/draw.h

The sentences taken from Go are reproduced from the doc comments of Go's `image/draw` package, Copyright 2009 The Go Authors, BSD-3-Clause.

- `ColorPalette draw_quantizer_quantize(DrawQuantizer q, ColorPalette p, Image m)` – Quantize appends up to cap(p) - len(p) colors to p and returns the updated palette suitable for converting m to a paletted image.
- `void draw_drawer_draw(DrawDrawer d, DrawImage dst, ImageRectangle r, Image src, ImagePoint sp)` – Draw aligns r.Min in dst with sp in src and then replaces the rectangle r in dst with the result of drawing src on dst.
- `void draw_op_draw(DrawOp op, DrawImage dst, ImageRectangle r, Image src, ImagePoint sp)` – Draw implements the Drawer interface by calling the Draw function with this Op.
- `DrawDrawer draw_op_as_drawer(const DrawOp *op)` – An Op as a Drawer.
- `extern const DrawDrawer draw_floyd_steinberg` – FloydSteinberg is a Drawer that is the Src Op with Floyd-Steinberg error diffusion.
- `void draw_draw(DrawImage dst, ImageRectangle r, Image src, ImagePoint sp, DrawOp op)` – Draw calls DrawMask with a nil mask.
- `void draw_draw_mask(DrawImage dst, ImageRectangle r, Image src, ImagePoint sp, Image mask, ImagePoint mp, DrawOp op)` – DrawMask aligns r.Min in dst with sp in src and mp in mask and then replaces the rectangle r in dst with the result of a Porter-Duff composition.

- `extern const Error io_eof` – EOF is the error returned by Read when no more input is available.
- `extern const Error io_err_unexpected_eof` – ErrUnexpectedEOF means that EOF was encountered in the middle of reading a fixed-size block or data structure.
- `extern const Error io_err_short_write` – ErrShortWrite means that a write accepted fewer bytes than requested but failed to return an explicit error.
- `extern const Error io_err_short_buffer` – ErrShortBuffer means that a read required a longer buffer than was provided.
- `extern const Error io_err_no_progress` – ErrNoProgress is returned by some clients of a Reader when many calls to Read have failed to return any data or error, usually the sign of a broken Reader implementation.
- `extern const Error io_err_closed_pipe` – ErrClosedPipe is the error used for read or write operations on a closed pipe.
- `Int io_read_full(IoReader r, Slice buf, Error *err)` – ReadFull reads exactly len(buf) bytes from r into buf.
- `Int io_read_at_least(IoReader r, Slice buf, Int min, Error *err)` – ReadAtLeast reads from r into buf until it has read at least min bytes.
- `int64_t io_copy_buffer(IoWriter dst, IoReader src, Slice buf, Error *err)` – CopyBuffer is identical to Copy except that it stages through the provided buffer (if one is required) rather than allocating a temporary one.
- `int64_t io_copy(Alloc *a, IoWriter dst, IoReader src, Error *err)` – Copy copies from src to dst until either EOF is reached on src or an error occurs.
- `int64_t io_copy_n(Alloc *a, IoWriter dst, IoReader src, int64_t n, Error *err)` – CopyN copies n bytes (or until an error) from src to dst.
- `Slice io_read_all(Alloc *a, IoReader r, Error *err)` – ReadAll reads from r until an error or EOF and returns the data it read.
- `Int io_write_string(IoWriter w, Str s, Error *err)` – WriteString writes the contents of the string s to w, which accepts a slice of bytes.
- `IoLimitedReader io_limit_reader(IoReader r, int64_t n)` – LimitReader returns a Reader that reads from r but stops with EOF after n bytes.

- `IoReader io_limited_reader_as_io_reader(IoLimitedReader *l)`

- `int64_t io_section_reader_seek(IoSectionReader *s, int64_t offset, Int whence, Error *err)`

- `int64_t io_section_reader_size(const IoSectionReader *s)` – Size returns the size of the section in bytes.

- `IoWriteSeeker io_offset_writer_as_io_write_seeker(IoOffsetWriter *o)`
- `IoTeeReader io_tee_reader(IoReader r, IoWriter w)` – TeeReader returns a Reader that writes to w what it reads from r.
- `IoReader io_tee_reader_as_io_reader(IoTeeReader *t)`
- `extern const IoWriter io_discard` – Discard is a Writer on which all Write calls succeed without doing anything.
- `IoNopCloser io_nop_closer(IoReader r)` – NopCloser returns a ReadCloser with a no-op Close method wrapping the provided Reader r.
- `IoReadCloser io_nop_closer_as_io_read_closer(IoNopCloser *c)`
- `IoReader io_multi_reader(Alloc *a, const IoReader *readers, Int n)` – MultiReader returns a Reader that's the logical concatenation of the provided input readers.
- `void io_multi_reader_free(Alloc *a, IoReader r)`
- `IoWriter io_multi_writer(Alloc *a, const IoWriter *writers, Int n)` – MultiWriter creates a writer that duplicates its writes to all the provided writers, similar to the Unix tee(1) command.
- `void io_multi_writer_free(Alloc *a, IoWriter w)`
- `void io_pipe(Alloc *a, IoPipeReader **r, IoPipeWriter **w)` – Pipe creates a synchronous in-memory pipe.
- `void io_pipe_free(IoPipeReader *r)`
- `Int io_pipe_reader_read(IoPipeReader *r, Slice data, Error *err)` – Read implements the standard Read interface: it reads data from the pipe, blocking until a writer arrives or the write end is closed.
- `Error io_pipe_reader_close(IoPipeReader *r)` – Close closes the reader; subsequent writes to the write half of the pipe will return the error ErrClosedPipe.
- `Error io_pipe_reader_close_with_error(IoPipeReader *r, Error err)` – CloseWithError closes the reader; subsequent writes to the write half of the pipe will return the error err.
- `Int io_pipe_writer_write(IoPipeWriter *w, Slice data, Error *err)` – Write implements the standard Write interface: it writes data to the pipe, blocking until one or more readers have consumed all the data or the read end is closed.
- `Error io_pipe_writer_close(IoPipeWriter *w)` – Close closes the writer; subsequent reads from the read half of the pipe will return no bytes and EOF.
- `Error io_pipe_writer_close_with_error(IoPipeWriter *w, Error err)` – CloseWithError closes the writer; subsequent reads from the read half of the pipe will return no bytes and the error err, or EOF if err is nil.

- `IoCloser io_pipe_reader_as_io_closer(IoPipeReader *r)`
- `IoReadCloser io_pipe_reader_as_io_read_closer(IoPipeReader *r)`
- `IoWriter io_pipe_writer_as_io_writer(IoPipeWriter *w)`
- `IoCloser io_pipe_writer_as_io_closer(IoPipeWriter *w)`

- `Str fs_read_link(Alloc *a, Fs fsys, Str name, Error *err)` – ReadLink returns the destination of the named symbolic link.

- `Slice fs_glob(Alloc *a, Fs fsys, Str pattern, Error *err)` – Glob returns the names of all files matching pattern or nil if there is no matching file.

- `extern const Error fs_err_exist` – "permission denied"
- `extern const Error fs_err_not_exist` – "file already exists"
- `extern const Error fs_err_closed` – ErrClosed is returned when operations are attempted on a file that has already been closed, including when Close is called more than once.
- `extern const Error fs_skip_dir` – SkipDir is used as a return value from WalkDirFunc to indicate that the directory named in the call is to be skipped.
- `extern const Error fs_skip_all` – SkipAll is used as a return value from WalkDirFunc to indicate that all remaining files and directories are to be skipped.
- `Error fs_walk_dir(Alloc *a, Fs fsys, Str root, FsWalkDirFunc fn)` – WalkDir walks the file tree rooted at root, calling fn for each file or directory in the tree, including root.
